#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read-only gh capture of upstream commits that landed after the pinned snapshot.

Freezes: the commit chain between the pinned snapshot and a newer upstream ref,
per-file Git blob hashes at both ends (verified against the blob object id), the
unified diff, a proof that the diff reproduces the newer blobs, and a check that
the local `CleanSlate/` tree still equals the pinned (older) end byte-for-byte in
its working-tree form (CP1252 + CRLF, per upstream `.gitattributes`).

No checkout, no source edits, no new snapshot. The newer tree is *described*,
not imported; offline checks live in `upstream_update_review.py`.
"""
import argparse
import base64
import csv
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

import charhist as ch
import crosscheck_upstream as up
import employer_claim_date_review as ed
from family_upstream import UPSTREAM, SNAPSHOT

REVIEW_DATE = '2026-10-02'
TEXT_SUFFIXES = ('.txt', '.csv', '.info')   # working-tree-encoding=Windows-1252, eol=crlf
BUGS_LINE = 'Number of noteworthy bugs and oversights fixed: '


def gh(args, cache, binary=False):
    """Cached `gh` call for non-REST shapes (raw diff, graphql, headers)."""
    key = up.digest(json.dumps(args).encode())
    p = cache/(key+('.bin' if binary else '.json'))
    if p.exists():
        return p.read_bytes() if binary else json.loads(p.read_text())
    raw = subprocess.check_output(['gh', *args], timeout=180)
    if binary:
        cache.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); p.write_bytes(raw); return raw
    result = json.loads(raw)
    ch.write_text(cache, p.name, json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True)+'\n')
    return result


def blob(sha, cache):
    meta = up.api('git/blobs/'+sha, cache)
    if meta.get('encoding') != 'base64': raise ValueError('unsupported blob encoding')
    raw = base64.b64decode(meta['content'], validate=False)
    if hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(raw)).encode()+b'\0'+raw).hexdigest() != sha:
        raise ValueError('blob integrity mismatch: '+sha)
    return raw


def worktree_form(raw, path):
    """Apply upstream .gitattributes: UTF-8/LF blob -> Windows-1252/CRLF file."""
    if not path.endswith(TEXT_SUFFIXES): return raw, 'binary/as-is'
    text = raw.decode('utf-8')
    if '\r\n' in text: raise ValueError('blob already CRLF: '+path)
    return text.replace('\n', '\r\n').encode('cp1252'), 'cp1252-crlf'


def tree_index(ref, cache):
    t = up.api('git/trees/'+ref+'?recursive=1', cache)
    if t.get('truncated'): raise ValueError('tree listing truncated for '+ref)
    blobs = {e['path']: e for e in t['tree'] if e['type'] == 'blob'}
    return t['sha'], blobs, sum(1 for e in t['tree'] if e['type'] == 'tree')


def history_count(ref, cache):
    q = ('query($o:String!,$n:String!,$e:String!){repository(owner:$o,name:$n)'
         '{object(expression:$e){... on Commit{history{totalCount}}}}}')
    owner, name = UPSTREAM.split('/')
    r = gh(['api', 'graphql', '-f', 'query='+q, '-f', 'o='+owner, '-f', 'n='+name, '-f', 'e='+ref], cache)
    return r['data']['repository']['object']['history']['totalCount']


def changelog_facts(raw):
    text = raw.decode('utf-8')
    lines = text.split('\n')
    bugs = next((int(l[len(BUGS_LINE):].strip()) for l in lines if l.startswith(BUGS_LINE)), None)
    renames = [l.strip()[2:] for l in lines if l.strip().startswith('- ') and ' -> ' in l]
    return {'header': lines[0].strip(), 'bugs_fixed': bugs, 'lines': len(lines) - (1 if text.endswith('\n') else 0),
            'bytes_blob': len(raw), 'bytes_worktree': len(worktree_form(raw, 'x.txt')[0]),
            'rename_lines': len(renames), 'rename_lines_list': renames}


def verify_diff(diff_bytes, files, base_blobs, head_blobs):
    """Apply the frozen diff to the base blobs in scratch space; must yield the head blobs."""
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)
        for f in files:
            if f['status'] != 'added':
                p = root/f['path']; p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); p.write_bytes(base_blobs[f['path']])
        (root/'_upstream.diff').write_bytes(diff_bytes)
        subprocess.run(['git', 'apply', '--whitespace=nowarn', '_upstream.diff'], cwd=root, check=True,
                       capture_output=True)
        for f in files:
            if f['status'] == 'removed':
                if (root/f['path']).exists(): return False, f['path']
            elif (root/f['path']).read_bytes() != head_blobs[f['path']]: return False, f['path']
    return True, None


def collect(head_ref, cache):
    repo = up.api('', cache) if False else json.loads(subprocess.check_output(['gh', 'api', 'repos/'+UPSTREAM]))
    main_ref = up.api('git/ref/heads/'+repo['default_branch'], cache)
    head_sha = up.api('commits/'+head_ref, cache)['sha']
    compare = up.api('compare/'+SNAPSHOT+'...'+head_sha, cache)
    if compare['total_commits'] != len(compare['commits']) or len(compare['files']) >= 300:
        raise ValueError('compare response is paginated/truncated; extend the collector')
    if compare['behind_by'] != 0: raise ValueError('head is not a descendant of the pinned snapshot')
    commits = []
    previous = SNAPSHOT
    for c in compare['commits']:
        full = up.api('commits/'+c['sha'], cache)
        parents = [p['sha'] for p in full['parents']]
        if parents != [previous]: raise ValueError('non-linear chain at '+c['sha'])
        previous = c['sha']
        commits.append({'sha': full['sha'], 'parents': parents, 'html_url': full['html_url'],
                        'author': full['commit']['author'], 'committer_date': full['commit']['committer']['date'],
                        'message': full['commit']['message'], 'stats': full['stats'],
                        'files': [{'filename': f['filename'], 'status': f['status'], 'additions': f['additions'],
                                   'deletions': f['deletions'], 'sha': f.get('sha')} for f in full['files']]})
    if previous != head_sha: raise ValueError('chain does not end at head')
    base_tree, base_index, base_dirs = tree_index(SNAPSHOT, cache)
    head_tree, head_index, head_dirs = tree_index(head_sha, cache)
    files, base_blobs, head_blobs = [], {}, {}
    for f in sorted(compare['files'], key=lambda f: f['filename']):
        path = f['filename']
        if f['status'] not in ('modified', 'added', 'removed'):
            raise ValueError('unsupported file status (rename/copy) needs explicit handling: '+path)
        entry = {'path': path, 'status': f['status'], 'additions': f['additions'], 'deletions': f['deletions'],
                 'base_blob': base_index.get(path, {}).get('sha'), 'head_blob': head_index.get(path, {}).get('sha')}
        if f['status'] != 'added' and entry['head_blob'] != (f['sha'] if f['status'] != 'removed' else None):
            raise ValueError('compare/tree blob disagreement: '+path)
        for end, index, store in (('base', base_index, base_blobs), ('head', head_index, head_blobs)):
            if path in index:
                raw = blob(index[path]['sha'], cache); store[path] = raw
                wt, form = worktree_form(raw, path)
                entry.update({end+'_size_blob': len(raw), end+'_raw_sha256': up.digest(raw),
                              end+'_worktree_sha256': up.digest(wt), end+'_size_worktree': len(wt), 'worktree_form': form})
        local = ch.REPO/'CleanSlate'/path
        entry['local_exists'] = local.exists()
        if local.exists():
            lb = local.read_bytes(); entry['local_sha256'] = up.digest(lb)
            entry['local_matches_base_worktree'] = entry.get('base_worktree_sha256') == up.digest(lb)
        else:
            entry['local_matches_base_worktree'] = f['status'] == 'added'
        if not entry['local_matches_base_worktree']:
            raise ValueError('local CleanSlate/ is not at the pinned snapshot for '+path)
        entry['in_character_history'] = path.startswith('CleanSlate/history/characters/')
        files.append(entry)
    diff_bytes = gh(['api', '-H', 'Accept: application/vnd.github.diff', 'repos/'+UPSTREAM+'/compare/'+SNAPSHOT+'...'+head_sha],
                    cache, binary=True)
    ok, failing = verify_diff(diff_bytes, files, base_blobs, head_blobs)
    if not ok: raise ValueError('frozen diff does not reproduce head blob: '+str(failing))
    readme = up.api('commits?path=README.md&per_page=1', cache)[0]
    changelog_path = 'CleanSlate/master_changelog.txt'
    changelog = {end: changelog_facts(store[changelog_path]) if changelog_path in store else
                 changelog_facts(blob(index[changelog_path]['sha'], cache))
                 for end, store, index in (('base', base_blobs, base_index), ('head', head_blobs, head_index))}
    tags = [{'name': t['name'], 'commit': t['commit']['sha']} for t in up.api('tags?per_page=5', cache)]
    releases = [{'tag': r['tag_name'], 'name': r['name'], 'published_at': r['published_at'], 'prerelease': r['prerelease']}
                for r in up.api('releases?per_page=5', cache)]
    pin_date = up.api('commits/'+SNAPSHOT, cache)['commit']['author']['date']
    issues = [{'number': i['number'], 'title': i['title'], 'state': i['state'], 'created_at': i['created_at'],
               'updated_at': i['updated_at'], 'user': i['user']['login'], 'is_pull_request': 'pull_request' in i,
               'comments': i['comments'], 'html_url': i['html_url']}
              for i in up.api('issues?state=all&sort=updated&direction=desc&since='+pin_date+'&per_page=100', cache)]
    return {
        'version': 1, 'review_date': REVIEW_DATE, 'retrieved_at_utc': datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        'upstream': UPSTREAM, 'pinned_snapshot': SNAPSHOT, 'pinned_snapshot_author_date': pin_date,
        'head': {'requested_ref': head_ref, 'sha': head_sha, 'default_branch': repo['default_branch'],
                 'default_branch_sha_at_capture': main_ref['object']['sha']},
        'repository': {'pushed_at': repo['pushed_at'], 'size_kb': repo['size'], 'open_issues_count': repo['open_issues_count'],
                       'html_url': repo['html_url']},
        'compare': {'status': compare['status'], 'ahead_by': compare['ahead_by'], 'behind_by': compare['behind_by'],
                    'total_commits': compare['total_commits'], 'files_changed': len(compare['files']),
                    'html_url': compare['html_url']},
        'history_total_count': {'base': history_count(SNAPSHOT, cache), 'head': history_count(head_sha, cache)},
        'chain_linear_single_parent': True, 'commits': commits, 'files': files,
        'trees': {'base': {'tree_sha': base_tree, 'blobs': len(base_index), 'directories': base_dirs},
                  'head': {'tree_sha': head_tree, 'blobs': len(head_index), 'directories': head_dirs}},
        'diff': {'sha256': up.digest(diff_bytes), 'bytes': len(diff_bytes), 'lines': diff_bytes.count(b'\n'),
                 'files': diff_bytes.count(b'\ndiff --git ') + diff_bytes.startswith(b'diff --git '),
                 'reproduces_head_blobs': ok},
        'readme_frontpage': {'path': 'README.md', 'last_commit': readme['sha'], 'date': readme['commit']['committer']['date'],
                             'subject': readme['commit']['message'].split('\n')[0]},
        'master_changelog': changelog, 'tags_latest': tags, 'releases_latest': releases, 'issues_since_pin': issues,
        'tools_sha256': {n: ch.sha(Path(__file__).with_name(n)) for n in
                         ('upstream_update_capture.py', 'crosscheck_upstream.py', 'family_upstream.py')},
        'limits': ('Describes commits between the pinned snapshot and the captured head only. Blob hashes are '
                   'verified Git object ids; local equality is asserted for the base end in working-tree form. '
                   'No engine run, no semantic certification of upstream changes, no new snapshot imported.')}


def write_tables(out, ev):
    buf = io.StringIO(); w = csv.writer(buf, lineterminator='\n')
    w.writerow(['order', 'sha', 'author_date', 'committer_date', 'author', 'files', 'additions', 'deletions', 'subject'])
    for i, c in enumerate(ev['commits'], 1):
        w.writerow([i, c['sha'][:8], c['author']['date'], c['committer_date'], c['author']['name'], len(c['files']),
                    c['stats']['additions'], c['stats']['deletions'], c['message'].split('\n')[0]])
    ch.write_text(out, 'commits.csv', buf.getvalue())
    buf = io.StringIO(); w = csv.writer(buf, lineterminator='\n')
    w.writerow(['path', 'status', 'additions', 'deletions', 'base_blob', 'head_blob', 'base_size_worktree', 'head_size_worktree',
                'local_matches_base_worktree', 'in_character_history', 'commits'])
    touched = {}
    for c in ev['commits']:
        for f in c['files']: touched.setdefault(f['filename'], []).append(c['sha'][:8])
    for f in ev['files']:
        w.writerow([f['path'], f['status'], f['additions'], f['deletions'], (f.get('base_blob') or '')[:12], (f.get('head_blob') or '')[:12],
                    f.get('base_size_worktree', ''), f.get('head_size_worktree', ''), f['local_matches_base_worktree'],
                    f['in_character_history'], ' '.join(touched.get(f['path'], []))])
    ch.write_text(out, 'files.csv', buf.getvalue())


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('--head', default='main', help='upstream ref to capture (default: main, resolved and pinned)')
    ap.add_argument('--cache', type=Path, default=Path('/home/user/ck2-upstream-update/api-cache'))
    ap.add_argument('--outdir', type=Path, default=ch.OUTDIR_DEFAULT/'upstream-update-2026-09')
    args = ap.parse_args(); out = ed.safe_destination(args.outdir); cache = ed.safe_destination(args.cache)
    cache.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    ev = collect(args.head, cache)
    diff_bytes = gh(['api', '-H', 'Accept: application/vnd.github.diff', 'repos/'+UPSTREAM+'/compare/'+SNAPSHOT+'...'+ev['head']['sha']],
                    cache, binary=True)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out/'upstream.diff').write_bytes(diff_bytes)
    ch.write_text(out, 'upstream-update-evidence.json', json.dumps(ev, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True)+'\n')
    write_tables(out, ev)
    print(json.dumps({'head': ev['head']['sha'], 'commits': len(ev['commits']), 'files': len(ev['files']),
                      'diff_reproduces_head_blobs': ev['diff']['reproduces_head_blobs'],
                      'local_at_pinned_base': all(f['local_matches_base_worktree'] for f in ev['files']),
                      'bugs_fixed': {k: v['bugs_fixed'] for k, v in ev['master_changelog'].items()}}, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__': main()

input.path:
/home/user/my-CK2-come/CleanSlate-Archive/06-session-records/tools/upstream_update_review.py
input.content:
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline review of the frozen upstream-update capture (no network).

Rebuilds the newer versions of the changed files by applying the frozen diff to
the local pinned snapshot, verifies them against the frozen Git blob ids, then
runs static checks at both ends: strict-reader parse, brace balance, event-id
collisions and dangling event references over the whole (swapped) tree, scripted
trigger/score-value definitions and their usage, new map-region county targets,
localisation key changes/duplicates/resolution, the new-game-lobby character
key/loc case match, changelog metrics, and the status of the archive's earlier
changelog findings. Static evidence only — not a CK2 run.

The archive's strict reader (`ck2script.py`) is kept byte-identical because a
published manifest pins its hash; event/trigger script uses the `==` comparator
that character history never does, so this tool normalises `==` to `=` before
parsing. Structural results (top-level keys, ids, braces) do not depend on it.
"""
import argparse
from collections import Counter, defaultdict
import csv
import hashlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import subprocess
import tempfile

import charhist as ch
from ck2script import ParseError, parse
import crosscheck_upstream as up
import employer_claim_date_review as ed

EVIDENCE_DIR = ch.OUTDIR_DEFAULT/'upstream-update-2026-09'
MOD = ch.REPO/'CleanSlate/CleanSlate'
VANILLA = ch.REPO/'Crusader Kings II'
EVENT_KEYS = {'character_event', 'narrative_event', 'letter_event', 'province_event',
              'diploresponse_event', 'long_character_event', 'unit_event', 'society_quest_event'}
EVENT_REF = re.compile(r'\b[a-z_]*event\s*=\s*\{[^{}]*?\bid\s*=\s*([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z_0-9]*\.\d+|\d+)')
EQ = re.compile(r'(?<=[\w\s"\]])==(?=[\w\s"\[])')
COMMENT = re.compile(r'"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|#[^\n]*')
SCRIPTED_DIRS = ('common/scripted_triggers', 'common/scripted_effects', 'common/scripted_score_values')
PROSE = {'CleanSlate/master_changelog.txt'}
# Archive findings that this update could have changed; each is re-tested below.
ARCHIVE_FINDINGS = [
    ('exec-1', 'README.md front page frozen since 2021-04-13', 'readme'),
    ('exec-2', "master_changelog.txt header still says 'beta 6.1'", 'header'),
    ('exec-3', "changelog typo 'd_wallachia -> c_muntenia' (should be d_muntenia)", 'rename:d_wallachia'),
    ('exec-4', "changelog typo 'd_pommerania -> c_pomerania' (should be d_pomerania)", 'rename:d_pommerania'),
    ('exec-5', 'EVTTOOLTIPHF20329 referenced in HF_coronation_events.txt, defined nowhere', 'loc:EVTTOOLTIPHF20329'),
    ('rep09/11/13', "changelog 'd_tangiers -> c_tangier' while replacements use d_tangier", 'rename:d_tangiers'),
    ('rename_rules', "changelog 'saxon -> anglo-saxon' vs implemented anglo_saxon", 'rename:saxon'),
    ('rename_rules', 'IMPL_RENAMES *_arabic/gujurati culture renames undocumented in changelog', 'rename:arabic'),
    ('timeline', 'latest release 1.0-beta6.2; no release since', 'release'),
]


def strip_comments(text):
    return COMMENT.sub(lambda m: m.group(0) if m.group(0).startswith('"') else '', text)


def parse_script(text, name):
    try:
        nodes, _ = parse(EQ.sub(' = ', text), name); return nodes, None
    except ParseError as e:
        return None, str(e)


def direct(node, key):
    return [c.value.value for c in node.value if not c.group and c.key.value == key] if node.group else []


def rebuild_head(ev, diff_bytes):
    """Local pinned files + frozen diff -> head texts; both ends verified by hash."""
    base, head = {}, {}
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)
        for f in ev['files']:
            local = ch.REPO/'CleanSlate'/f['path']
            raw = local.read_bytes()
            if up.digest(raw) != f['base_worktree_sha256']: raise ValueError('local file moved off the pinned base: '+f['path'])
            blob = raw.decode('cp1252').replace('\r\n', '\n').encode('utf-8')
            if hashlib.sha1(b'blob %d\0' % len(blob)+blob).hexdigest() != f['base_blob']:
                raise ValueError('local file does not round-trip to the base blob: '+f['path'])
            base[f['path']] = blob.decode('utf-8')
            p = root/f['path']; p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); p.write_bytes(blob)
        (root/'_u.diff').write_bytes(diff_bytes)
        subprocess.run(['git', 'apply', '--whitespace=nowarn', '_u.diff'], cwd=root, check=True, capture_output=True)
        for f in ev['files']:
            blob = (root/f['path']).read_bytes()
            if hashlib.sha1(b'blob %d\0' % len(blob)+blob).hexdigest() != f['head_blob']:
                raise ValueError('rebuilt head file does not match frozen blob id: '+f['path'])
            head[f['path']] = blob.decode('utf-8')
    return base, head


class Tree:
    """Read-only view of the mod with changed files swapped to one end."""
    def __init__(self, texts):
        self.texts = texts
    def read(self, rel):
        key = 'CleanSlate/'+rel
        return self.texts[key] if key in self.texts else ch.read_text(MOD/rel)
    def files(self, rel_dir, suffix='.txt'):
        return sorted(p.relative_to(MOD).as_posix() for p in (MOD/rel_dir).glob('*'+suffix))
    def all_txt(self):
        return sorted(p.relative_to(MOD).as_posix() for p in MOD.rglob('*.txt'))


def event_health(tree):
    defined, where, errors = Counter(), defaultdict(list), []
    for rel in tree.files('events'):
        nodes, err = parse_script(tree.read(rel), rel)
        if err: errors.append(err); continue
        for n in nodes:
            if n.key.value in EVENT_KEYS:
                for i in direct(n, 'id'): defined[i] += 1; where[i].append(rel)
    refs = Counter()
    for rel in tree.all_txt():
        if rel.startswith('history/'): continue
        for m in EVENT_REF.finditer(strip_comments(tree.read(rel))): refs[m.group(1)] += 1
    dangling = sorted(r for r in refs if r not in defined)
    return {'defined_ids': len(defined), 'duplicate_ids': sorted(i for i, c in defined.items() if c > 1),
            'parse_errors': errors, 'referenced_ids': len(refs), 'dangling': dangling, 'where': where}


def scripted_defs(tree):
    defs = defaultdict(list)
    for d in SCRIPTED_DIRS:
        for rel in tree.files(d):
            nodes, err = parse_script(tree.read(rel), rel)
            if err: raise ValueError(err)
            for n in nodes: defs[n.key.value].append(rel)
    return defs


def loc_keys(tree):
    keys, where = Counter(), defaultdict(list)
    for rel in tree.files('localisation', '.csv'):
        for line in tree.read(rel).split('\n'):
            if not line or line.startswith('#'): continue
            k = line.split(';', 1)[0].strip()
            if k and k != 'CODE' and k != '\ufeffCODE': keys[k] += 1; where[k].append(rel)
    return keys, where


def csv_entries(text):
    out = {}
    for line in text.split('\n'):
        if line and not line.startswith('#'):
            k, _, rest = line.partition(';'); out[k.strip()] = rest
    return out


def lobby_check(defs_text, info_text, lockey_set):
    defs = re.findall(r'(?m)^([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*\{', strip_comments(defs_text))
    refs = [w for m in re.findall(r'interesting_characters\s*=\s*\{([^}]*)\}', strip_comments(info_text)) for w in m.split()]
    rows = []
    for d in defs:
        rows.append({'definition': d, 'referenced_exact': d in refs, 'referenced_case_insensitive': d.lower() in {r.lower() for r in refs},
                     'desc_loc_exact': ('DLC_INTERESTING_CHAR_%s_DESC' % d) in lockey_set,
                     'desc_loc_case_insensitive': ('DLC_INTERESTING_CHAR_%s_DESC' % d).lower() in {k.lower() for k in lockey_set}})
    return rows, [r for r in refs if r not in defs]


def write_csv(out, name, header, rows):
    buf = io.StringIO(); w = csv.writer(buf, lineterminator='\n'); w.writerow(header); w.writerows(rows)
    ch.write_text(out, name, buf.getvalue())


def review(ev, diff_bytes, out):
    base_t, head_t = rebuild_head(ev, diff_bytes)
    base, head = Tree(base_t), Tree(head_t)
    results = {'engine_executed': False, 'files': []}
    # 1. per-file parse / braces / event ids
    file_rows = []
    for f in ev['files']:
        path = f['path']; rel = path[len('CleanSlate/'):]
        row = {'path': path, 'status': f['status']}
        if path.endswith('.txt') and path not in PROSE:
            for end, t in (('base', base_t[path]), ('head', head_t[path])):
                nodes, err = parse_script(t, path)
                s = strip_comments(t); row[end+'_parse'] = 'ok' if err is None else err
                row[end+'_brace_delta'] = s.count('{') - s.count('}')
                if rel.startswith('events/') and nodes is not None:
                    row[end+'_event_ids'] = sorted(i for n in nodes if n.key.value in EVENT_KEYS for i in direct(n, 'id'))
        file_rows.append(row); results['files'].append(row)
    write_csv(out, 'changed-files-review.csv',
              ['path', 'status', 'base_parse', 'head_parse', 'base_brace_delta', 'head_brace_delta', 'event_ids_added', 'event_ids_removed'],
              [[r['path'], r['status'], r.get('base_parse', ''), r.get('head_parse', ''), r.get('base_brace_delta', ''), r.get('head_brace_delta', ''),
                ' '.join(sorted(set(r.get('head_event_ids', [])) - set(r.get('base_event_ids', [])))),
                ' '.join(sorted(set(r.get('base_event_ids', [])) - set(r.get('head_event_ids', []))))] for r in file_rows])
    # 2. whole-tree event health at both ends
    eh = {end: event_health(t) for end, t in (('base', base), ('head', head))}
    results['event_health'] = {end: {k: v for k, v in r.items() if k != 'where'} for end, r in eh.items()}
    # 3. scripted definitions: duplicates, new names, usage of new names
    sd = {end: scripted_defs(t) for end, t in (('base', base), ('head', head))}
    new_names = sorted(set(sd['head']) - set(sd['base'])); removed_names = sorted(set(sd['base']) - set(sd['head']))
    all_head_text = {rel: strip_comments(head.read(rel)) for rel in head.all_txt() if not rel.startswith('history/')}
    def uses(name):
        pat = re.compile(r'(?<![\w.])'+re.escape(name)+r'(?![\w])')
        return {rel: len(pat.findall(t)) for rel, t in all_head_text.items() if pat.search(t)}
    def_rows = []
    for n in new_names:
        u = uses(n); defining = sd['head'][n]
        outside = {r: c for r, c in u.items() if r not in defining}
        def_rows.append([n, ' '.join(defining), sum(outside.values()), len(outside), ' '.join(sorted(outside))])
    results['scripted'] = {'definitions_base': sum(len(v) for v in sd['base'].values()), 'definitions_head': sum(len(v) for v in sd['head'].values()),
                           'duplicate_names_base': sorted(k for k, v in sd['base'].items() if len(v) > 1),
                           'duplicate_names_head': sorted(k for k, v in sd['head'].items() if len(v) > 1),
                           'new_names': new_names, 'removed_names': removed_names,
                           'new_names_unused_outside_definition': [r[0] for r in def_rows if r[2] == 0]}
    write_csv(out, 'scripted-definitions.csv', ['name', 'defined_in', 'uses_outside_definition', 'files_using', 'using_files'], def_rows)
    # 4. new geographical regions -> county targets exist; region used; loc key present
    titles = set()
    for rel in head.files('common/landed_titles'):
        titles |= set(re.findall(r'(?m)^\s*([ekdcb]_[A-Za-z0-9_\-]+)\s*=\s*\{', strip_comments(head.read(rel))))
    hist_titles = {p.stem for p in (MOD/'history/titles').glob('*.txt')}
    region_rows = []; regions = {}
    for end, t in (('base', base), ('head', head)):
        nodes, err = parse_script(t.read('map/geographical_region.txt'), 'geographical_region.txt')
        if err: raise ValueError(err)
        regions[end] = {n.key.value: n for n in nodes}
    lk_head, lk_where_head = loc_keys(head)
    new_regions = sorted(set(regions['head']) - set(regions['base']))
    for r in new_regions:
        node = regions['head'][r]
        counties = [c.key.value for g in node.value if g.group and g.key.value == 'counties' for c in g.value]
        for c in counties:
            region_rows.append([r, c, c in titles, c in hist_titles])
        results.setdefault('regions', {})[r] = {'counties': len(counties), 'missing_in_landed_titles': [c for c in counties if c not in titles],
                                                'missing_title_history': [c for c in counties if c not in hist_titles],
                                                'uses_outside_definition': {k: v for k, v in uses(r).items() if k != 'map/geographical_region.txt'},
                                                'loc_key_present_head': r in lk_head}
    write_csv(out, 'region-counties.csv', ['region', 'county', 'in_landed_titles', 'has_title_history'], region_rows)
    # 5. localisation: per-file key changes, global duplicates, resolution of loc keys named by changed script
    lk_base, _ = loc_keys(base)
    loc_rows = []
    for f in ev['files']:
        if f['path'].endswith('.csv'):
            b, h = csv_entries(base_t[f['path']]), csv_entries(head_t[f['path']])
            for k in sorted(set(h) - set(b)): loc_rows.append([f['path'], k, 'added', h[k].split(';')[0]])
            for k in sorted(set(b) - set(h)): loc_rows.append([f['path'], k, 'removed', b[k].split(';')[0]])
            for k in sorted(set(b) & set(h)):
                if b[k] != h[k]: loc_rows.append([f['path'], k, 'changed', h[k].split(';')[0]])
    write_csv(out, 'localisation-keys.csv', ['file', 'key', 'change', 'english_after'], loc_rows)
    named = Counter()
    for path, t in head_t.items():
        if path.endswith('.txt') and path not in PROSE:
            for key in ('title', 'title_female', 'foa', 'foa_female', 'localisation_key', 'nickname', 'name', 'short_name'):
                for v in re.findall(r'(?m)^\s*'+key+r'\s*=\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_\-\.]*)\s*(?:#.*)?$', strip_comments(t)):
                    if key in ('name',) and not path.endswith(('cb_types.txt', 'cbs.txt', 'cb.txt')): continue
                    named[(path, key, v)] += 1
    # Only keys that are new relative to the base end of the same file are judged (upstream delta, not a full loc audit).
    named_base = Counter()
    for path, t in base_t.items():
        if path.endswith('.txt') and path not in PROSE:
            for key in ('title', 'title_female', 'foa', 'foa_female', 'localisation_key', 'nickname', 'name', 'short_name'):
                for v in re.findall(r'(?m)^\s*'+key+r'\s*=\s*([A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_\-\.]*)\s*(?:#.*)?$', strip_comments(t)):
                    if key in ('name',) and not path.endswith(('cb_types.txt', 'cbs.txt', 'cb.txt')): continue
                    named_base[(path, key, v)] += 1
    new_refs = sorted(k for k in named if k not in named_base)
    ref_rows = [[p, k, v, v in lk_head, v in lk_base] for p, k, v in new_refs]
    write_csv(out, 'new-loc-references.csv', ['file', 'field', 'key', 'defined_head', 'defined_base'], ref_rows)
    results['localisation'] = {'keys_base': sum(lk_base.values()), 'keys_head': sum(lk_head.values()),
                               'duplicate_keys_base': sorted(k for k, c in lk_base.items() if c > 1),
                               'duplicate_keys_head': sorted(k for k, c in lk_head.items() if c > 1),
                               'per_file_changes': Counter(r[2] for r in loc_rows),
                               'new_script_loc_references': len(ref_rows), 'new_script_loc_references_unresolved': [r[:3] for r in ref_rows if not r[3]]}
    # 6. lobby characters: key/reference/loc case agreement at base, head and vanilla
    van_lk = set()
    for p in (VANILLA/'localisation').glob('*.csv'):
        for line in ch.read_text(p).split('\n'):
            if line and not line.startswith('#'): van_lk.add(line.split(';', 1)[0].strip())
    lobby = {}
    for end, defs_text, info_text, lks in (
            ('vanilla', ch.read_text(VANILLA/'dlc_metadata/dlc_characters/00_dlc_characters.txt'), ch.read_text(VANILLA/'dlc_metadata/dlc_info.txt'), van_lk),
            ('base', base_t['CleanSlate/dlc_metadata/dlc_characters/00_dlc_characters.txt'], base_t['CleanSlate/dlc_metadata/dlc_info.txt'], set(lk_base)),
            ('head', head_t['CleanSlate/dlc_metadata/dlc_characters/00_dlc_characters.txt'], head_t['CleanSlate/dlc_metadata/dlc_info.txt'], set(lk_head))):
        rows, unmatched_refs = lobby_check(defs_text, info_text, lks)
        lobby[end] = {'definitions': len(rows), 'references_not_matching_a_definition_exact': unmatched_refs,
                      'definitions_without_exact_desc_loc': [r['definition'] for r in rows if not r['desc_loc_exact']],
                      'definitions_with_case_only_desc_loc_mismatch': [r['definition'] for r in rows if not r['desc_loc_exact'] and r['desc_loc_case_insensitive']],
                      'rows': rows}
    results['lobby_characters'] = {end: {k: v for k, v in d.items() if k != 'rows'} for end, d in lobby.items()}
    write_csv(out, 'lobby-characters.csv', ['tree', 'definition', 'referenced_exact', 'referenced_case_insensitive', 'desc_loc_exact', 'desc_loc_case_insensitive'],
              [[end, r['definition'], r['referenced_exact'], r['referenced_case_insensitive'], r['desc_loc_exact'], r['desc_loc_case_insensitive']]
               for end, d in lobby.items() for r in d['rows']])
    # 7. changelog metrics and README gap (method: non-blank stripped lines of the changelog absent from README.md)
    readme_lines = {l.strip() for l in ch.read_text(ch.REPO/'CleanSlate/README.md').split('\n') if l.strip()}
    cl = {}
    for end, t in (('base', base_t['CleanSlate/master_changelog.txt']), ('head', head_t['CleanSlate/master_changelog.txt'])):
        lines = [l.strip() for l in t.split('\n') if l.strip()]
        cl[end] = {'nonblank_lines': len(lines), 'lines_absent_from_readme': sum(1 for l in lines if l not in readme_lines),
                   'bugs_fixed': ev['master_changelog'][end]['bugs_fixed'], 'header': ev['master_changelog'][end]['header'],
                   'rename_lines': ev['master_changelog'][end]['rename_lines']}
    rb, rh = ev['master_changelog']['base']['rename_lines_list'], ev['master_changelog']['head']['rename_lines_list']
    cl['rename_lines_added'] = sorted(set(rh) - set(rb)); cl['rename_lines_removed'] = sorted(set(rb) - set(rh))
    results['changelog'] = cl
    # 8. archive findings re-tested at head
    head_cl = head_t['CleanSlate/master_changelog.txt']
    status = []
    for fid, text, probe in ARCHIVE_FINDINGS:
        if probe == 'readme':
            s = 'still open' if ev['readme_frontpage']['last_commit'].startswith('43b93a64') else 'changed'
            e = 'README.md last commit %s (%s)' % (ev['readme_frontpage']['last_commit'][:8], ev['readme_frontpage']['date'][:10])
        elif probe == 'header':
            s = 'still open' if cl['head']['header'] == 'CleanSlate beta 6.1' else 'changed'; e = 'header: '+cl['head']['header']
        elif probe.startswith('rename:'):
            key = probe.split(':', 1)[1]
            if key == 'arabic':
                want = ['maghreb_arabic -> berber', 'andalusian_arabic -> andalusian', 'bedouin_arabic -> bedouin', 'egyptian_arabic -> egyptian',
                        'levantine_arabic -> levantine', 'gujurati -> gujarati']
                have = [w for w in want if w in rh]; s = 'resolved upstream' if len(have) == len(want) else 'partly'; e = '%d/%d now documented' % (len(have), len(want))
            else:
                lines = [l for l in rh if l.startswith(key+' -> ')]; old = [l for l in rb if l.startswith(key+' -> ')]
                s = 'resolved upstream' if lines != old else 'still open'; e = 'base: %s | head: %s' % (old, lines)
        elif probe.startswith('loc:'):
            key = probe.split(':', 1)[1]; s = 'still open' if key not in lk_head else 'resolved upstream'
            e = 'defined in head loc: %s; HF_coronation_events.txt changed in this update: %s' % (key in lk_head, any(f['path'].endswith('HF_coronation_events.txt') for f in ev['files']))
        elif probe == 'release':
            s = 'still open' if ev['releases_latest'][0]['tag'] == '1.0-beta6.2' else 'changed'; e = 'latest release '+ev['releases_latest'][0]['tag']+' '+ev['releases_latest'][0]['published_at'][:10]
        status.append([fid, text, s, e])
    write_csv(out, 'archive-findings-status.csv', ['finding', 'text', 'status_at_head', 'evidence'], status)
    results['archive_findings'] = Counter(r[2] for r in status)
    results['character_history_paths_changed'] = [f['path'] for f in ev['files'] if f['in_character_history']]
    return results


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('--evidence', type=Path, default=EVIDENCE_DIR)
    ap.add_argument('--outdir', type=Path, default=None, help='defaults to the evidence folder')
    args = ap.parse_args(); out = ed.safe_destination(args.outdir or args.evidence)
    ev = json.loads((args.evidence/'upstream-update-evidence.json').read_text())
    diff_bytes = (args.evidence/'upstream.diff').read_bytes()
    if up.digest(diff_bytes) != ev['diff']['sha256']: raise ValueError('frozen diff hash mismatch')
    results = review(ev, diff_bytes, out)
    results['inputs'] = {'evidence_sha256': ch.sha(args.evidence/'upstream-update-evidence.json'), 'diff_sha256': ev['diff']['sha256'],
                         'readme_sha256': ch.sha(ch.REPO/'CleanSlate/README.md')}
    results['tools_sha256'] = {n: ch.sha(Path(__file__).with_name(n)) for n in ('upstream_update_review.py', 'upstream_update_capture.py', 'ck2script.py')}
    outputs = ['changed-files-review.csv', 'scripted-definitions.csv', 'region-counties.csv', 'localisation-keys.csv', 'new-loc-references.csv',
               'lobby-characters.csv', 'archive-findings-status.csv']
    results['outputs_sha256'] = {n: ch.sha(out/n) for n in outputs}
    results['limits'] = ('Static checks on rebuilt head files and the swapped local tree. Parse uses the strict reader after == normalisation. '
                         'Usage counts are word matches in comment-stripped script, not semantic reachability. No engine run.')
    ch.write_text(out, 'review.json', json.dumps(results, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True, default=str)+'\n')
    summary = {'event_health': {e: {k: (len(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v) for k, v in r.items()} for e, r in results['event_health'].items()},
               'scripted': {k: (len(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v) for k, v in results['scripted'].items()},
               'localisation': {k: (len(v) if isinstance(v, list) else v) for k, v in results['localisation'].items()},
               'regions': results.get('regions'), 'lobby': results['lobby_characters'], 'changelog': results['changelog'],
               'archive_findings': results['archive_findings'], 'character_history_paths_changed': results['character_history_paths_changed'],
               'parse_failures': [(r['path'], r.get('head_parse')) for r in results['files'] if r.get('head_parse', 'ok') != 'ok']}
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, default=str))


if __name__ == '__main__': main()

input.content:
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validate the frozen upstream-update capture and its offline review (no network).

Checks: evidence/diff hashes agree; the commit chain is linear and ends at the
captured head; every changed local file still equals the pinned base end in
working-tree form; the frozen diff re-applied to the local files reproduces the
frozen head blob ids; the review outputs hash as published; and the headline
counts the report quotes are the ones in the artifacts. Writes
`audits/upstream-update-validation.json`. Never claims CK2 execution.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile

import charhist as ch
import crosscheck_upstream as up
import upstream_update_review as rv


def validate(evidence_dir):
    ev = json.loads((evidence_dir/'upstream-update-evidence.json').read_text())
    review = json.loads((evidence_dir/'review.json').read_text())
    diff = (evidence_dir/'upstream.diff').read_bytes()
    assert up.digest(diff) == ev['diff']['sha256'] == review['inputs']['diff_sha256'], 'diff hash drift'
    assert review['inputs']['evidence_sha256'] == ch.sha(evidence_dir/'upstream-update-evidence.json'), 'evidence hash drift'
    assert ev['pinned_snapshot'] == up.SNAPSHOT
    previous = ev['pinned_snapshot']
    for c in ev['commits']:
        assert c['parents'] == [previous], 'non-linear chain'; previous = c['sha']
    assert previous == ev['head']['sha'] and ev['compare']['total_commits'] == len(ev['commits'])
    assert ev['history_total_count']['head'] - ev['history_total_count']['base'] == len(ev['commits'])
    assert len(ev['files']) == ev['compare']['files_changed'] == ev['diff']['files']
    for name, digest in review['tools_sha256'].items():
        assert ch.sha(Path(__file__).with_name(name)) == digest, 'tool changed since review: '+name
    for name, digest in review['outputs_sha256'].items():
        assert ch.sha(evidence_dir/name) == digest, 'review output changed: '+name
    # Local pinned base still intact, and the frozen diff still rebuilds the head blobs.
    local_ok = 0
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)
        for f in ev['files']:
            raw = (ch.REPO/'CleanSlate'/f['path']).read_bytes()
            assert up.digest(raw) == f['base_worktree_sha256'] == f['local_sha256'], 'local snapshot drifted: '+f['path']
            blob = raw.decode('cp1252').replace('\r\n', '\n').encode('utf-8')
            assert hashlib.sha1(b'blob %d\0' % len(blob)+blob).hexdigest() == f['base_blob']
            p = root/f['path']; p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True); p.write_bytes(blob); local_ok += 1
        (root/'_u.diff').write_bytes(diff)
        subprocess.run(['git', 'apply', '--whitespace=nowarn', '_u.diff'], cwd=root, check=True, capture_output=True)
        for f in ev['files']:
            blob = (root/f['path']).read_bytes()
            assert hashlib.sha1(b'blob %d\0' % len(blob)+blob).hexdigest() == f['head_blob'], 'head rebuild mismatch: '+f['path']
    # Headline figures quoted by report 15.
    eh = review['event_health']; sc = review['scripted']; lo = review['localisation']; cl = review['changelog']
    assert eh['base']['duplicate_ids'] == eh['head']['duplicate_ids'] == [] and eh['base']['dangling'] == eh['head']['dangling'] == []
    assert sc['duplicate_names_head'] == [] and sc['new_names_unused_outside_definition'] == [] and len(sc['new_names']) == 4
    assert lo['duplicate_keys_head'] == [] and lo['new_script_loc_references_unresolved'] == []
    assert cl['base']['bugs_fixed'] == 661 and cl['head']['bugs_fixed'] == 908 and cl['base']['lines_absent_from_readme'] == 922
    assert review['character_history_paths_changed'] == []
    assert all(r.get('head_parse', 'ok') == 'ok' and r.get('head_brace_delta', 0) == 0 for r in review['files'])
    return {'status': 'passed', 'engine_executed': False, 'upstream_head': ev['head']['sha'], 'pinned_snapshot': ev['pinned_snapshot'],
            'commits': len(ev['commits']), 'files_changed': len(ev['files']), 'local_files_at_pinned_base': local_ok,
            'diff_rebuilds_head_blobs': True, 'review_outputs_checked': len(review['outputs_sha256']),
            'evidence_sha256': review['inputs']['evidence_sha256'], 'diff_sha256': ev['diff']['sha256'],
            'review_sha256': ch.sha(evidence_dir/'review.json'),
            'event_duplicates_head': 0, 'event_dangling_head': 0, 'loc_duplicates_head': 0,
            'bugs_fixed': {'base': 661, 'head': 908}, 'archive_findings': review['archive_findings'],
            'limits': 'Hash/consistency validation of frozen static evidence; not a semantic or runtime certification of upstream changes.'}


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('--evidence', type=Path, default=rv.EVIDENCE_DIR)
    ap.add_argument('--audit', type=Path, default=ch.OUTDIR_DEFAULT/'audits'/'upstream-update-validation.json')
    args = ap.parse_args()
    result = validate(args.evidence)
    ch.write_text(args.audit.parent, args.audit.name, json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True))


if __name__ == '__main__': main()

input.content:
"""Regression tests for the upstream-update capture/review helpers (offline)."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import unittest

import charhist as ch
import upstream_update_capture as cap
import upstream_update_review as rv

EVIDENCE = rv.EVIDENCE_DIR


class WorktreeForm(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_text_blob_becomes_cp1252_crlf(self):
        raw = 'a;Maj\u00e9st\u00e9\nb\n'.encode('utf-8')
        out, form = cap.worktree_form(raw, 'x/00_test.csv')
        self.assertEqual(form, 'cp1252-crlf')
        self.assertEqual(out, b'a;Maj\xe9st\xe9\r\nb\r\n')

    def test_non_text_suffix_is_left_alone(self):
        self.assertEqual(cap.worktree_form(b'\x89PNG\n', 'gfx/x.png'), (b'\x89PNG\n', 'binary/as-is'))

    def test_crlf_blob_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            cap.worktree_form(b'a\r\nb\r\n', 'x.txt')


class ChangelogFacts(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_header_count_and_rename_lines(self):
        raw = ('CleanSlate beta 6.1\nNumber of noteworthy bugs and oversights fixed: 908\n\n= Titles =\n'
               ' - d_wallachia -> d_muntenia\n - d_tangiers -> d_tangier\nnot a rename\n').encode('utf-8')
        facts = cap.changelog_facts(raw)
        self.assertEqual(facts['header'], 'CleanSlate beta 6.1')
        self.assertEqual(facts['bugs_fixed'], 908)
        self.assertEqual(facts['rename_lines_list'], ['d_wallachia -> d_muntenia', 'd_tangiers -> d_tangier'])
        self.assertEqual(facts['lines'], 7)


class ReviewHelpers(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_double_equals_is_normalised_for_the_strict_reader(self):
        nodes, err = rv.parse_script('t = {\n\tlimit = { month_of_birth == 3 }\n\tday_of_birth >= 20\n}\n', 't')
        self.assertIsNone(err)
        self.assertEqual(nodes[0].key.value, 't')

    def test_strip_comments_keeps_quoted_hashes(self):
        self.assertEqual(rv.strip_comments('a = "x # y" # real comment\n'), 'a = "x # y" \n')

    def test_event_reference_pattern(self):
        text = 'character_event = { id = HF.100 days = 3 }\nprovince_event = { id = 2001 }\n'
        self.assertEqual([m.group(1) for m in rv.EVENT_REF.finditer(text)], ['HF.100', '2001'])

    def test_lobby_check_flags_case_only_mismatch(self):
        rows, unmatched = rv.lobby_check('Ioannes = {\n\tid = 1\n}\nfernando = {\n\tid = 2\n}\n',
                                         'dlcs = { x = { interesting_characters = { ioannes fernando } } }',
                                         {'DLC_INTERESTING_CHAR_Ioannes_DESC', 'DLC_INTERESTING_CHAR_Fernando_DESC'})
        by = {r['definition']: r for r in rows}
        self.assertEqual(unmatched, ['ioannes'])
        self.assertTrue(by['Ioannes']['desc_loc_exact'])
        self.assertFalse(by['fernando']['desc_loc_exact'])
        self.assertTrue(by['fernando']['desc_loc_case_insensitive'])


@unittest.skipUnless((EVIDENCE/'upstream-update-evidence.json').exists(), 'frozen evidence not present')
class FrozenEvidence(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.ev = json.loads((EVIDENCE/'upstream-update-evidence.json').read_text())

    def test_chain_is_linear_from_pin_to_head(self):
        previous = self.ev['pinned_snapshot']
        for c in self.ev['commits']:
            self.assertEqual(c['parents'], [previous]); previous = c['sha']
        self.assertEqual(previous, self.ev['head']['sha'])

    def test_local_snapshot_is_still_the_pinned_base(self):
        for f in self.ev['files']:
            self.assertEqual(ch.sha(ch.REPO/'CleanSlate'/f['path']), f['base_worktree_sha256'], f['path'])

    def test_diff_hash_matches(self):
        self.assertEqual(ch.sha(EVIDENCE/'upstream.diff'), self.ev['diff']['sha256'])


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()

output.status:

import re, pathlib
root=pathlib.Path('CleanSlate/CleanSlate')
pat=re.compile(r'\b(NOT|NOR)\s*=\s*\{\s*(?:#[^\n]*\s*)?trigger_if\s*=\s*\{', re.S)
hits=[]
for p in sorted(root.rglob('*.txt')):
    if 'history/' in p.as_posix(): continue
    t=p.read_bytes().decode('cp1252',errors='replace')
    # strip comments
    t=re.sub(r'"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|#[^\n]*', lambda m: m.group(0) if m.group(0).startswith('"') else '', t)
    for m in pat.finditer(t):
        line=t.count('\n',0,m.start())+1
        # does the NOT/NOR contain anything besides the trigger_if? find the block end
        i=m.start(); depth=0; j=t.index('{',i)
        k=j
        while True:
            if t[k]=='{': depth+=1
            elif t[k]=='}':
                depth-=1
                if depth==0: break
            k+=1
        body=t[j+1:k]
        # count top-level children in body
        d=0; children=0; token=False
        for ch_ in body:
            if ch_=='{': d+=1
            elif ch_=='}': d-=1
            elif ch_=='=' and d==0: children+=1
        hits.append((p.relative_to(root).as_posix(), line, m.group(1), children))
print("NOT/NOR blocks that open directly with trigger_if (local tree = pinned base):")
for h in hits: print("  %s:%d  %s = { trigger_if ... }  top-level children=%d%s" % (h[0],h[1],h[2],h[3], "  <-- trigger_if is the ONLY child" if h[3]==1 else ""))
print("total:",len(hits), " only-child cases:", sum(1 for h in hits if h[3]==1))
EOF

def loc_keys(tree):
input.new_text:
NOT_TRIGGER_IF = re.compile(r'\b(NOT|NOR|NAND)\s*=\s*\{\s*trigger_if\s*=\s*\{')


def not_trigger_if_scan(tree):
    """`NOT = { trigger_if = { limit … } }` with no sibling: an unmet limit leaves the NOT
    empty, which evaluates true, so the NOT is always false (upstream 8bf3eef9's note)."""
    rows = []
    for rel in tree.all_txt():
        if rel.startswith('history/'): continue
        t = strip_comments(tree.read(rel))
        for m in NOT_TRIGGER_IF.finditer(t):
            j = t.index('{', m.start()); depth = 0; k = j
            while True:
                if t[k] == '{': depth += 1
                elif t[k] == '}':
                    depth -= 1
                    if depth == 0: break
                k += 1
            body = t[j+1:k]; d = 0; children = 0
            for c in body:
                if c == '{': d += 1
                elif c == '}': d -= 1
                elif c == '=' and d == 0: children += 1
            rows.append([rel, t.count('\n', 0, m.start())+1, m.group(1), children, children == 1])
    return rows


def loc_keys(tree):

EOF

# Upstream update check — twelve commits after the pinned snapshot

**2026-10-02 · `ck2plus/CleanSlate` `7ee3cbae` → `40058a01` · static evidence, not an engine test**

[Previous: transcript provenance](14-transcript-provenance.md) ·
[Evidence guide](upstream-update-2026-09/README.md) ·
[Validation](audits/upstream-update-validation.json) · [Remaining work](04-open-work.md)

## What this check establishes

Upstream `main` moved for the first time since the snapshot this archive is built on.
The movement was captured, verified blob-by-blob, re-measured with the archive's health
checks, and compared against the archive's own published findings. **The local snapshot
was not changed** — `CleanSlate-main.zip` and the unpacked `CleanSlate/` tree are still
byte-exact at `7ee3cbae`; the newer tree is *described* (frozen diff + Git object ids),
not imported. Whether to refresh the snapshot is an open decision ([§6](#6-effect-on-this-archive)).

- **Twelve commits, one author (Whizzer), 2026-09-26 → 2026-09-30, a linear chain**
  from `7ee3cbae` to `40058a01` (every commit has exactly one parent, the previous
  commit). 39 files, all *modified* (none added, renamed or removed), +1,045/−1,295
  lines. Repository history 2,172 → 2,184 commits. No new tag or release — the
  latest is still `1.0-beta6.2` (2025-06-05).
- **None of the 39 files is under `history/characters/`.** The character-history
  comparison (reports 06–13) reads inputs this update did not touch; its outputs
  and validators are unaffected and still pass (152 tests).
- **Health at the new head is unchanged** — recomputed over the whole mod tree with
  the 39 files swapped to their new versions: 10,272 event IDs, **0** duplicates,
  **0** dangling references; scripted definitions 1,615 → 1,619 with **0** duplicate
  names and all four new names in use; localisation 92,142 → 92,148 keys with **0**
  duplicates and all six new script-side references resolving. All 36 changed
  script files parse under the archive's strict reader and balance braces at both ends.
- **The live changelog finally caught up.** `master_changelog.txt` grew +338/−23
  lines (105 KB → 136 KB); its headline "noteworthy bugs and oversights fixed" went
  **661 → 908**. That single commit **resolves five findings this archive had
  published** — three changelog typos, the `anglo-saxon`/`anglo_saxon` spelling and
  the undocumented `*_arabic` culture renames — while four remain open: the GitHub
  front-page `README.md` is still frozen at `43b93a64` (2021-04-13), the changelog
  header still reads "beta 6.1", `EVTTOOLTIPHF20329` is still undefined, and there
  is still no release.
- **Two static observations worth sending upstream** came out of the check
  ([§5](#5-observations-for-upstream)): the new-game-lobby fix restores vanilla casing
  on two character keys but leaves `Fernando_of_castille` without a case-exact
  description key, and one new localisation row lacks the customary `x` terminator.

Status vocabulary follows [INDEX.md](INDEX.md#status-vocabulary): everything below is
**Verified** against the frozen evidence unless marked Observed or Open.

## 1. What landed

| # | Commit | Date (author) | Files | +/− | Subject |
|--:|---|---|--:|---|---|
| 1 | [`6d08ccf8`](https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/commit/6d08ccf8) | 09-26 | 15 | +326/−1,116 | Casus belli maintenance |
| 2 | [`52f61eb1`](https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/commit/52f61eb1) | 09-26 | 5 | +164/−63 | Title management fixes |

| 5 | [`a00b4c81`](https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/commit/a00b4c81) | 09-28 | 2 | +5/−5 | Fixed the 'slayer of \<culture\>' nickname referring to one's own culture |

| 12 | [`40058a01`](https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/commit/40058a01) | 09-30 | 1 | +338/−23 | Updated master changelog |

Commits 1–4 share one committer timestamp (`15:54:46Z`) and 5–6 another: they were
rebased/pushed in batches. Full messages, per-commit file lists and both-end blob ids
are in [`upstream-update-evidence.json`](upstream-update-2026-09/upstream-update-evidence.json);
the whole change is [`upstream.diff`](upstream-update-2026-09/upstream.diff) (136 KB).

By area: `common/` 19 files (12 of them `cb_types/`), `events/` 8, `decisions/` 4,
`localisation/` 4, `dlc_metadata/` 2, `map/` 1, plus the changelog.

### What the larger commits actually do

- **Casus belli maintenance (1)** is the bulk of the deletions: −1,120/+161 across the
  twelve `cb_types` files. Long per-title eligibility lists were replaced by
  `region =` checks (`world_steppe`, `world_middle_east`, `world_europe_east_russia`,
  `world_india`, … and two custom regions), and repeated blocks were extracted into
  three new scripted triggers (`suitable_holdings_trigger`,
  `temujin_avoid_china_trigger`, `defender_controls_enough_land_trigger`) and one
  scripted score value (`horde_guidance_factor`). A new region **`custom_novgorod`**
  (15 counties) was added to `map/geographical_region.txt` with its localisation row,
  and is used once, in `pagan_cbs.txt`. All 15 counties exist in `landed_titles` and
  have title history. The "Viking guidance" now also lists `world_europe_west_gaul`.
- **Title management fixes (2)** touches four decisions/events files: decision-formed
  kingdoms are now assigned a de jure empire, custom kingdoms/empires take the
  creator's capital as de jure capital (`capital_scope = { save_event_target_as =
  current_capital }` → `set_preferred_capital`), the restored-Rome decision uses
  `set_name = b_roma` instead of a literal `Rome`, and the crusader titular-duchy
  handling moved into event form.
- **Female cultural titles (4)** adds `title_female` entries for Condottiero,
  Grandmaster, Grand Headmaster, Hochmeister and Warchief, plus five localisation keys
  (`CONDOTTIERA`, `GRANDMASTER_FEMALE`, `GRANDHEADMASTER_FEMALE`, `HOCHMEISTERIN`,
  `WARCHIEF_FEMALE`); every new reference resolves.
- **Religion features (11)** rewrites four triggers of the form
  `NOT = { trigger_if = { limit = {…} X } }` into
  `trigger_if = { limit = {…} NOT = { X } }`. Whizzer's explanation: a `trigger_if`
  whose limit is unmet contributes nothing, leaving the `NOT` empty — an empty trigger
  is true, so the `NOT` was always false. A tree-wide scan finds exactly **four**
  blocks where `trigger_if` was the only child of a `NOT` at the base end — the four
  fixed — and **zero** at head ([`not-trigger-if-scan.csv`](upstream-update-2026-09/not-trigger-if-scan.csv));
  the remaining 13 `NOR = { trigger_if … }` blocks all have sibling conditions, which is
  the case Whizzer describes as safe.
- **Papal AI (7)** is a one-liner that upstream itself labels "CleanSlate bug": the
  +30 "faith threatened" crusade modifier fired on `religion_authority >= 0.25`
  instead of `NOT = { religion_authority >= 0.25 }`.
- **Nicknames (5)** renames the saved target so decision and localisation agree on
  `nickname_target` (zero `nickname_location` references remain at head); the commit
  message describes the direction the other way round, but the result is consistent
  with how `adventurer_cbs`/`nomad_cbs` already name it.
- **Flyting insult (6)** swaps `ROOT`/`FROM` in two customizable-localisation
  triggers; Whizzer cited it on upstream issue
  [#221](https://github.com/ck2plus/CleanSlate/issues/221) on 2026-09-30 (still open,
  awaiting the reporter).

## 2. How it was verified

Three tools, following the archive's capture / offline review / validator pattern
([tools/README.md](tools/README.md)):

```bash
cd CleanSlate-Archive/06-session-records/tools
python3 upstream_update_capture.py --head 40058a01689eb08d252027df1fd89ec9c53a9e9a   # network, read-only
python3 upstream_update_review.py                                                     # offline
python3 validate_upstream_update.py                                                   # offline
python3 -m unittest discover -s . -p 'test_*.py'                                      # 152 tests
```

The capture asserts, and the evidence records, that:

1. the GitHub compare is complete (12 of 12 commits, 39 files, not paginated), the
   chain is single-parent and linear from the pin to the head, and the head equals
   `main` at capture time;
2. every changed file's Git blob at **both** ends was fetched and its object id
   recomputed (`sha1("blob <len>\0" + bytes)`), so blob ids are verified, not trusted;
3. after applying upstream's `.gitattributes` contract (UTF-8/LF in Git →
   Windows-1252/CRLF in the working tree), the **base-end bytes equal the local
   `CleanSlate/` files for all 39 paths** — the local snapshot is provably still at
   `7ee3cbae`;
4. `git apply` of the frozen diff onto those base blobs reproduces all 39 head blobs
   byte-for-byte, so the 136 KB diff is sufficient evidence for the new state.

The offline review rebuilds the head files from local files + diff (re-verifying both
blob ids), swaps them into a read-only view of the mod tree and runs the checks in §3.
Two runs produce byte-identical `review.json`. The validator re-performs the hash and
chain checks and pins the headline numbers quoted here.

One method note: the archive's strict Clausewitz reader (`ck2script.py`) is pinned by
hash in `comparison/comparison-run.json`, so it was not modified. Event and trigger
script uses the `==` comparator that character history never does; the review tool
normalises `==` to `=` before parsing. Top-level keys, ids and brace counts do not
depend on that.

## 4. The changelog update and this archive's findings

`master_changelog.txt` at `40058a01`: 2,360 lines (was 2,045), header still
`CleanSlate beta 6.1`, 441 rename lines (was 434). Non-blank lines absent from the
frozen front-page `README.md`: **922 → 1,223** — the base figure reproduces the
archive's published "922 lines" exactly, so the two numbers are comparable.

|---|---|---|
| Front-page `README.md` frozen since 2021-04-13 | exec. summary #1, [changelog audit](../04-changelog/changelog-audit.md) | **still open** — last commit `43b93a64`; now 12 more commits behind, 11 of them script |
| `master_changelog.txt` header says "beta 6.1" | exec. summary #2 | **still open** |

| `saxon -> anglo-saxon` documented vs `anglo_saxon` implemented | [`rename_rules.py`](tools/rename_rules.py) `DOC_RENAMES`/`IMPL_RENAMES` split | **resolved upstream** (`saxon -> anglo_saxon`) |

The same commit also corrected five rename-table *keys* the archive had not flagged
(`d_abkhaziad`→`d_abkhazia`, `d_tioharad`→`d_varend`, `d_samogitia`→`d_scalovia`,
`d_yaroslavl`→`d_vologda`, `e_kanem`→`e_kanem-bornu` — the new-name side of each
"old: X -> Y" line) and added `d_lappish_band -> d_sami_band`. Nothing in the
`DOC_RENAMES` table needs to change for the archive's comparison to stay valid: that
table deliberately pins the *documented spellings as of `7ee3cbae`*, and the
implementation side was already curated correctly. If the snapshot is ever refreshed,
`DOC_RENAMES` should be re-derived from the new changelog (the `IMPL_RENAMES`
overrides for `saxon`, the `*_arabic` set and `gujurati` would then become redundant).

Whether Whizzer saw this archive or found the same typos independently is not knowable
from the evidence and is not claimed either way; the archive's repository is private.

## 5. Observations for upstream

Static, file-level; none is a runtime claim.

1. **Lobby characters — one case-only mismatch moved rather than disappeared.**
   The engine derives the new-game-lobby description key from the character
   definition key (`DLC_INTERESTING_CHAR_<key>_DESC`); vanilla keeps both capitalised
   (`Ioannes`, `Fernando_of_castille`) and all 60 references match their definitions
   exactly. CleanSlate's house style had lowercased the two definitions; for
   `Ioannes` the loc key stayed capitalised (hence the character not showing — the
   bug `94b45345` fixes), while for `Fernando_of_castille` CleanSlate had lowercased
   definition, reference *and* loc key consistently. `94b45345` recapitalises both
   definitions, but `00_dlc.csv` still carries `DLC_INTERESTING_CHAR_fernando_of_castille_DESC`.
   Result at head: `Fernando_of_castille` is now the definition without a case-exact
   description key, and the `dlc_info.txt` reference for Ioannes is lowercase
   (`ioannes`) while its definition is `Ioannes`. If the lookups are case-sensitive
   (Whizzer's commit message says leading capitals matter), the Fernando description
   will fall back to the raw key. Full matrix for vanilla/base/head in
   [`lobby-characters.csv`](upstream-update-2026-09/lobby-characters.csv).
   *Suggested fix:* rename the loc key to `DLC_INTERESTING_CHAR_Fernando_of_castille_DESC`
   and reference `Ioannes` with its capital, i.e. restore vanilla on all three sides.
2. **`GRANDHEADMASTER_FEMALE` has no trailing `x`.** The other five new rows end
   `;x` like 99.7 % of the file set (278 of 92,142 rows already lack it, mostly
   rows with trailing comments). Cosmetic. Observed.
3. **`CONDOTTIERA;Condottiere;…`** — the English column of the new female key uses
   the masculine form; the Spanish column has `Condotiera`. Possibly intentional
   English usage. Observed, not a defect claim.
4. The commit message of `a00b4c81` describes the `nickname_target`/`nickname_location`
   direction inversely to the diff. The code is consistent; only the message is off.

## 6. Effect on this archive

What is now stale, and what is not:

| Claim in the archive | Status |
|---|---|
| "`CleanSlate-main.zip` is byte-identical to upstream `7ee3cbae`" | **still true** (re-verified for all 39 changed paths) |
| "`7ee3cbae` is upstream `HEAD`" (archive README, [method.md](../05-findings/method.md)) | **no longer true** — HEAD is `40058a01`; wording updated to "pinned snapshot" |
| "2,172 commits, 2019-12-18 → 2026-06-29" | now 2,184, → 2026-09-30 (12 by Whizzer) |
| "661+ documented bug fixes" | upstream's own count is now **908** (same header line, same file) |
| "983 commits changed game script since the README froze" | 994 by the same definition (+11) |
| "922 lines only in `master_changelog.txt`" | 1,223 at head (same method; 922 reproduced at base) |
| Character-history comparison, reports 06–13, 139 → 152 tests | **unaffected**; inputs untouched |
| Changelog audit's two typos; Tangier `c_`/`d_`; `rename_rules` doc/impl split | **resolved upstream** — annotated, not rewritten |

**Snapshot refresh is deliberately not done here.** Replacing `CleanSlate-main.zip`
and the unpacked tree would change the zip hash that `charhist.py extract` and the
comparison manifests pin, require regenerating `comparison/` and re-deriving
`DOC_RENAMES`, and turn every "at `7ee3cbae`" statement into a migration. The 39
changed files do not include any character history, so the comparison would come
out identical — but that should be shown by rerunning it, not assumed. The decision
and the exact procedure are queued in [04-open-work.md](04-open-work.md).

## Limits

- Static file evidence only; nothing was executed in CK2. "Unused", "resolves" and
  "dangling" are word/key matches in comment-stripped script, not reachability or
  tooltip rendering.
- The compare covers `7ee3cbae..40058a01` on `main`. Other branches, forks and PR
  heads (e.g. open PR #238) were listed but not analysed.
- Upstream's `README.md`, `master_changelog.txt` sections and the 908 count are
  reported as upstream states them; the 247 newly counted fixes were not individually
  re-verified against commits.
- Parse results use the strict reader after `==` normalisation (see §2).

    `path='CleanSlate', since=<README freeze>` reproduces the archive's "983 script commits".\"\"\"

# Upstream update evidence — `7ee3cbae` → `40058a01` (captured 2026-10-02)

[Report 15](../15-upstream-update-2026-09.md) ·
[Validation](../audits/upstream-update-validation.json) · [Queue](../04-open-work.md)

Two separate processes, kept distinct as in the earlier upstream captures:

1. **Network capture** (`tools/upstream_update_capture.py`): read the GitHub compare
   between the pinned snapshot and the newer head, fetch every commit, both trees and
   both blobs of every changed file (object ids recomputed), fetch the unified diff,
   prove the diff rebuilds the head blobs, and assert the local `CleanSlate/` files
   equal the base end in working-tree form. No checkout, no source edits.
2. **Offline review** (`tools/upstream_update_review.py`): rebuild the head files
   from local files + frozen diff, swap them into a read-only view of the mod and run
   static checks at both ends. Deterministic; two runs are byte-identical.

Neither is a CK2 run. **The local snapshot was not updated.**

## Files

| File | Rows / coverage | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| [upstream-update-evidence.json](upstream-update-evidence.json) | 12 commits, 39 files, 2 trees | Frozen capture: compare, full commit messages, per-file blob ids/sizes/hashes at both ends, local equality, diff hash, README/changelog/tag/release/issue state |
| [upstream.diff](upstream.diff) | 4,526 lines, 136 KB | GitHub's unified diff for the whole range (UTF-8/LF, Git-side encoding) |
| [commits.csv](commits.csv) | 12 | Order, author/committer dates, size, subject |
| [files.csv](files.csv) | 39 | Path, status, +/−, blob ids, working-tree sizes, local match, touching commits |
| [review.json](review.json) | — | All offline results, input/tool/output hashes |
| [changed-files-review.csv](changed-files-review.csv) | 39 | Strict-reader parse and brace balance at both ends; event ids added/removed |
| [scripted-definitions.csv](scripted-definitions.csv) | 4 | New scripted trigger/score-value names and where they are used |
| [region-counties.csv](region-counties.csv) | 15 | `custom_novgorod` counties vs `landed_titles` and title history |
| [localisation-keys.csv](localisation-keys.csv) | 8 | Key-level changes in the three changed CSVs |
| [new-loc-references.csv](new-loc-references.csv) | 6 | Loc keys newly named by changed script, and whether they resolve |
| [lobby-characters.csv](lobby-characters.csv) | 186 | `dlc_characters` key / `dlc_info` reference / `DLC_INTERESTING_CHAR_*_DESC` case agreement for vanilla, base and head |
| [archive-findings-status.csv](archive-findings-status.csv) | 9 | Each earlier archive finding re-tested at the head |
| [not-trigger-if-scan.csv](not-trigger-if-scan.csv) | 30 | Every `NOT/NOR/NAND = { trigger_if … }` block at both ends, with sibling counts |

## Interpretation rules

- Blob ids are verified Git object ids. Local equality is asserted on the base end in
  working-tree form (Windows-1252 + CRLF per upstream `.gitattributes`), not on raw
  blob bytes, which are UTF-8/LF.
- The head state exists here only as "local file + diff"; `review.json` records that
  the rebuilt files hash to the frozen head blob ids. If the local snapshot is ever
  refreshed, this folder stays valid as the record of the `7ee3cbae..40058a01` step.
- Parse results come from the archive's strict reader after normalising the `==`
  comparator (the reader is pinned by hash elsewhere and was not modified).
- "Used", "resolves" and "dangling" are word/key matches in comment-stripped script.
  Nothing here is a semantic or runtime verdict.

## Reproduction

```bash
cd CleanSlate-Archive/06-session-records/tools
python3 upstream_update_capture.py --head 40058a01689eb08d252027df1fd89ec9c53a9e9a   # gh, read-only; cache in /home/user/ck2-upstream-update
python3 upstream_update_review.py
python3 validate_upstream_update.py
```

Re-running the capture against a *later* upstream head is supported (`--head main`),
but write to a new `--outdir` so this frozen record is kept.

## Scope decisions (2026-09-21)

- **The analysis is for the repository owner's personal use.** Upstream
  outreach is not planned; the "help the author" idea was motivational, and
  the archive's value is the understanding itself. If outreach is ever
  revisited, it still requires fresh verification and explicit user
  authorization — the guard below is unchanged.
- Session transcripts are shredded into the knowledge base and deleted after
  extraction; see [14-transcript-provenance.md](14-transcript-provenance.md).

## Deliverables and provenance

Current generated evidence is indexed in [INDEX.md](INDEX.md); commands and
limits are in [03-methods-and-reproduction.md](03-methods-and-reproduction.md).
`comparison-run.json` fingerprints the generator, input provinces, ZIP and outputs;
`step1-file-inventory.csv` fingerprints all character inputs.

The old reports/tool remain in Git at
`d7fe54ef96db60c3023fd9835481dd93cc3cffa2`. The historical audit intentionally
reproduces that baseline. The recheck tables remain historical source evidence;
consult the audit/family review for corrected interpretation of “fixed”/“missing.”

## Do not do

- Do not modify vanilla, CleanSlate, `characters/`, `character_sheets/`, or source ZIPs.
- Do not renumber IDs, merge trees, or invent replacement genealogy.
- Do not trust count parity as proof of a rename or runtime equivalence.
- Do not restrict field-specific scans to the current 66 residual files.
- Do not overwrite full-install manifests with hashes of the stripped snapshot.
- Do not report an upstream bug based only on transcripts or normalized diffs.

# Transcript provenance — the shred map (2026-09-21)

> **What happened to the session transcripts.** The nine root-level log files
> were ripped apart, every piece of value sorted into this knowledge base, and
> the emptied originals deleted from the working tree. They remain recoverable
> from Git history (they are tracked in `78de990`, e.g.
> `git show 78de990:log4.txt`).

## Why shredding was safe now

The policy was set in session 4 (`log2.txt`): curate the transcripts into
navigable notes, keep the originals temporarily, and delete them only after a
fresh verification pass confirmed nothing important was missed. That
precondition is met:

- the [interruption audit](06-interruption-audit.md) checked the log3 sessions
  claim-by-claim against the files and Git history;
- the comparison tool was rebuilt on a strict parser and tested (139 tests,
  two-seed byte-identical outputs);
- recovery checks confirmed the extracted CleanSlate tree, the 17 repairs and
  the 62 character sheets against their sources;
- this shred pass itself re-verified every load-bearing observation before
  moving it into the curated notes (see [verification corrections](#verification-corrections-made-during-this-shred) below).

## The nine transcripts and where their content lives

| Transcript | Session | Repo¹ | What it covered | Content now lives in |

=== 01-history/timeline.md head ===
# Project history

← [Archive index](../README.md)

Reconstructed from the upstream git history: 2,172 commits, **2019-12-18 → 2026-06-29**.

## The four eras

### 1. The import (Dec 2019 – mid 2020) — "make it ours"

The project began by pulling vanilla into the repo *folder by folder*, each as its own
commit, with the state recorded in the message:

```
2019-12-18  Initial commit
2019-12-18  alternate_start (Already Clean)
2019-12-18  artifact_spawns (Already Clean)
2020-02-26  Scripted triggers: import
2020-02-26  Save conversion: emptied out
2020-03-03  Import Traits
2020-04-01  Import: gfx and interface folders
2020-06-13  Import: Province History
2020-06-30  Import: Map
```

Two things stand out. `"(Already Clean)"` means they *audited* each folder and recorded
the verdict rather than silently copying. And the import ran for **four and a half years**
— `Imported war history` lands in Nov 2022, `Tutorial: import` in Apr 2024. The migration
was incremental by design, not a big-bang fork.

**2020 is the peak year: 808 commits.**

### 2. Alpha → beta (Jul 2020 – Apr 2021)

`1.0-alpha` is tagged 2020-07-27 after **784 commits**. `1.0-beta` follows 2021-04-13
after another 375. The README/Master Changelog is written at this point — and, as it
turns out, never substantially updated again. See
[§5 changelog audit](../04-changelog/changelog-audit.md).

### 3. Rapid beta iteration (2021 – 2022)

## Convention going forward

New session transcripts arrive at the repository root as uploads. Shred them
the same way: extract anything not already captured (findings → `02`,
methods → `03`, side observations → `05`, chronology → `01`, one row in the
table above), then delete the file. Do not store raw transcripts inside the
knowledge base — the routing map plus Git history is the provenance chain.
=== 01-history/timeline tail ===
numbers**, so outside contributions land through review. Both issue templates are
customised, and the feature-request one encodes project scope as policy.

### Finding: a split identity

`Schwarherz` (267) and `schwarherz` (141) are **the same person** — both
`schwarherz@gmail.com` — and appear as two separate rows in every contributor list,
including GitHub's. Merged they total **408**, making them clearly the **second** largest
contributor rather than third *and* fourth.

There is no `.mailmap`. Adding one fixes every future `git shortlog`, `git log --author`,
and contributor graph:

```gitattributes
# .mailmap
Schwarherz <schwarherz@gmail.com> schwarherz <schwarherz@gmail.com>
Elizabeth <holloway.elizabeth16@gmail.com> Legate-Elizabeth <143746601+Legate-Elizabeth@users.noreply.github.com>
```

(The second line is a likely — not certain — match: `Elizabeth` and `Legate-Elizabeth`
are distinct emails, so confirm with the contributor before merging.)

## Bus-factor note

Contribution is extremely concentrated, and the pattern of one-year contributors
(Silversweeper 2024, nbmsc 2023, Sergei Ivanov 2021–23) suggests drive-by help rather than
a standing team. Combined with the fact that the **house style is documented only in
commit messages** — there is no `CONTRIBUTING.md` — the cost of onboarding a new
maintainer is higher than it needs to be. See
[§4 Conventions](../03-conventions/style-guide.md), which reconstructs those rules.

Contract: the capture recomputes every blob's Git object id, asserts the local
`CleanSlate/` files equal the base end in working-tree form (CP1252/CRLF per upstream
`.gitattributes`), and proves `git apply` of the frozen diff reproduces the head blobs.

tree view for the health checks; it normalises `==` before using `ck2script.py`, which
stays unmodified because `comparison/comparison-run.json` pins its hash. Evidence and
tables: [`upstream-update-2026-09/`](upstream-update-2026-09/README.md). To watch for

input.command:
cd /home/user/my-CK2-come && echo "=== protected trees untouched? ===" && git status --short -- CleanSlate "Crusader Kings II" characters character_sheets CleanSlate-main.zip | wc -l && cd CleanSlate-Archive/06-session-records/tools && python3 validate_upstream_update.py | grep '"status"' && python3 -m unittest discover -s . -p 'test_*.py' 2>&1 | grep -E '^Ran|^OK|FAIL' && cd /home/user/my-CK2-come && python3 - <<'EOF'
import re, pathlib, subprocess
files = subprocess.check_output(['git','status','--short']).decode().split('\n')
mds=[]
for l in files:
    if not l.strip(): continue
    path=l[3:].strip()
    p=pathlib.Path(path)
    if p.is_dir(): mds += [str(x) for x in p.rglob('*.md')]
    elif path.endswith('.md'): mds.append(path)
bad=[]
for md in mds:
    text=pathlib.Path(md).read_text()
    for m in re.finditer(r'\]\(([^)\s]+)\)', text):
        target=m.group(1)
        if target.startswith(('http://','https://','#','mailto:')): continue
        target=target.split('#')[0]
        if not target: continue
        resolved=(pathlib.Path(md).parent/target)
        if not resolved.exists(): bad.append((md,target))
print("markdown files checked:",len(mds)); print("broken relative links:",bad if bad else "none")
EOF
